Dated this the 26th day of September 2019
JUDGMENT
K.VINOD CHANDRAN,J.
We had passed an interim order dated
19.09.2019 in some of the above cases seeking
police protection, which is as follows:
State Attorney takes notice for the
respondents 1 to 4.
Sri.S.Krishnamoorthy takes notice for
the respondents 5 and 6 in all these
writ petitions.
2. The learned Counsel appearing
for the respondents 5 and 6 submits that
they intend no obstruction to the

W.P.(C) No.24682/2019
& connected cases ..217..
willing workers of the various branches.
In such circumstance, if there is any
obstruction caused to the willing
workers of the branches, then the Police
shall interfere and remove such
obstruction. It is made clear that this
will not interfere with the right of the
striking workers to peacefully carry on
protest against the Management in front
of the branches, but, however, not
creating any obstruction to the smooth
functioning of the branches.
3. It is submitted by the learned
State Attorney that there is a
conciliation initiated by the State
authorities, in which the Management is
not cooperating. It is also submitted
that the Management themselves had filed
a writ petition seeking conciliation
proceedings in the matter. The said
writ petition is numbered as W.P(C)
No.22610/2019. The Court itself had
suggested mediation in the said writ
petition.
4. The Management shall ensure that
due participation is made in any
conciliation. It shall also be ensured
that none other than the persons who are
in the muster roll of a branch shall be
employed in a particular branch.
Post on 25.09.2019 along with
W.P(C) No.22610/2019.

W.P.(C) No.24682/2019
& connected cases ..218..
2. We make the interim order absolute in
all these cases. We make it clear that the
Managers of the various branches can approach
the Station House Officer if there is any
disruption caused to the smooth functioning
of the branches. However, the specifications
indicated in the interim order will be
scrupulously followed by the Managment. If
round the clock protection is required and
police personnel are specifically posted, the
Government shall be entitled to raise a bill
on the branch Manager as provided under G.O
(P) No.16/13/Fin., dated, 07.01.2013. We also
make it clear that this order would be
subject to the co-operation of the Management
in the conciliation as would be directed in
W.P.(C) No.22610 of 2019 and the further
orders passed in that writ petition.

W.P.(C) No.24682/2019
& connected cases ..219..
The above writ petitions are disposed of
subject to the above reservation. Parties to
suffer their respective costs.

 Sd/-
 K. VINOD CHANDRAN
 Judge.
 Sd/-
 V.G.ARUN,
 Judge.

& connected cases ..220..
